## Judging convention used in these files (recorded, NOT decided — merger must pick one rule
project-wide and apply it to every databinding batch)
Generated *Binding/*BindingImpl source and DataBinderMapperImpl are NOT in final_java_only
and were NOT printed, so NO row in these files is SOURCE: every REACHABLE row is INFERRED,
even where the app-side inflate/setViewModel call site itself is printed, because the step
from DataBindingUtil.inflate to the Impl is generated-mapper dispatch and the step from a
base-class call to the Impl override is generated virtual dispatch. This follows batch 67's
convention (and batches 62-64/68-70 on the same points) and is applied uniformly to 65-67:
- Impl <clinit> / both <init> overloads / base <init> / executeBindings / invalidateAll:
  REACHABLE INFERRED on a printed inflate of the exact layout (mapper instantiation and
  generated-constructor rebind are the standard generated pattern, body not printed).
- hasPendingBindings: OPEN everywhere — no printed app or framework caller in the printed
  corpus; claiming a framework call without a printed generated/framework body would be
  invention.
- onFieldChange / setVariable: REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where a variable is actually set in
  printed app code (a setViewModel call for a layout declaring that variable) and the layout
  binds observables of it; where no variable is set (layouts with NO <data> block), both stay
  OPEN. This is the batch 67 rule.
- onChangeX (nested binding): REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where the parent layout includes child
  X AND the child layout binds observables that change in use; where the child layout has no
  <data>/observables, onChangeX stays OPEN (batch 66 row 7, appbar).
- Base getViewModel: OPEN everywhere in these files (see convention (v) below) — the
  executeBindings read path reads the mViewModel field, not the getter, as far as can be
  told without the generated body.
- setLifecycleOwner: OPEN everywhere — zero hits in all 26 freshly fetched files.
- Impl$1/$2 callback numbering (batch 66 rows 36-39): by generated-source order, NOT
  bytecode-confirmed.
- OPEN rows carry confirmed_by INFERRED in the table only as the ceiling of what can be said
  without generated source / a saved full-repo grep; it is NOT a label claim and NOT evidence
  of absence. No row is UNREACHABLE in these files, because no saved full-repo grep was run.

### Table D — setVariable direction contradiction, batches 63 / 64 / 65 (NOT decided)

Contradiction, flagged never decided: batch 65 treats Impl setVariable as REACHABLE via
"generated dispatch point behind binding.setViewModel", which is mechanically backwards
— in generated code setVariable CALLS the typed setter (setViewModel), not vice versa,
so a printed binding.setViewModel() call does not by itself trigger Impl setVariable.
Batches 63-64 keep setVariable OPEN on zero printed setVariable hits. Batches 63, 64
and 65 (and, for consistency, 66-68) must use ONE rule:

| Option | 65/3, 65/16, 65/27, 65/38 (currently REACHABLE, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 63/10, 63/24, 64/4, 64/13, 64/22, 64/31 (currently OPEN, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 66/10, 67/8, 68/1 and other no-variable setVariable rows (currently OPEN) |
|--------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| D1 — batch-65 current rule (setViewModel implies setVariable dispatch) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | stay OPEN under (xv) |
| D2 — strict mechanical rule (only a printed setVariable call, or a printed generated body, triggers Impl setVariable) | revert to OPEN | stay OPEN | stay OPEN |
| D3 — generic-framework-callback rule (framework/generated dispatch always runs setVariable) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED — but this conflicts with (xv) and the merger must reconcile D3 with (xv) explicitly |

This pass applies none of D1/D2/D3 to change a label: the four batch-65 REACHABLE
setVariable rows are left exactly as written and flagged here, and the 63/64 rows are
not edited (outside this group). A printed generated Impl setVariable/setViewModel
body would settle the direction mechanically and supersede this table.

## Judging convention used in these files (recorded, NOT decided — merger must pick one rule
project-wide and apply it to every databinding batch)
Generated *Binding/*BindingImpl source and DataBinderMapperImpl are NOT in final_java_only
and were NOT printed, so NO row in these files is SOURCE: every REACHABLE row is INFERRED,
even where the app-side inflate/setViewModel call site itself is printed, because the step
from DataBindingUtil.inflate to the Impl is generated-mapper dispatch and the step from a
base-class call to the Impl override is generated virtual dispatch. This follows batch 67's
convention (and batches 62-64/68-70 on the same points) and is applied uniformly to 65-67:
- Impl <clinit> / both <init> overloads / base <init> / executeBindings / invalidateAll:
  REACHABLE INFERRED on a printed inflate of the exact layout (mapper instantiation and
  generated-constructor rebind are the standard generated pattern, body not printed).
- hasPendingBindings: OPEN everywhere — no printed app or framework caller in the printed
  corpus; claiming a framework call without a printed generated/framework body would be
  invention.
- onFieldChange / setVariable: REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where a variable is actually set in
  printed app code (a setViewModel call for a layout declaring that variable) and the layout
  binds observables of it; where no variable is set (layouts with NO <data> block), both stay
  OPEN. This is the batch 67 rule.
- onChangeX (nested binding): REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where the parent layout includes child
  X AND the child layout binds observables that change in use; where the child layout has no
  <data>/observables, onChangeX stays OPEN (batch 66 row 7, appbar).
- Base getViewModel: OPEN everywhere in these files (see convention (v) below) — the
  executeBindings read path reads the mViewModel field, not the getter, as far as can be
  told without the generated body.
- setLifecycleOwner: OPEN everywhere — zero hits in all 26 freshly fetched files.
- Impl$1/$2 callback numbering (batch 66 rows 36-39): by generated-source order, NOT
  bytecode-confirmed.
- OPEN rows carry confirmed_by INFERRED in the table only as the ceiling of what can be said
  without generated source / a saved full-repo grep; it is NOT a label claim and NOT evidence
  of absence. No row is UNREACHABLE in these files, because no saved full-repo grep was run.

   and Sw600dpLand Impls are instantiated only under matching device configurations. Both

### Table D — setVariable direction contradiction, batches 63 / 64 / 65 (NOT decided)

Contradiction, flagged never decided: batch 65 treats Impl setVariable as REACHABLE via
"generated dispatch point behind binding.setViewModel", which is mechanically backwards
— in generated code setVariable CALLS the typed setter (setViewModel), not vice versa,
so a printed binding.setViewModel() call does not by itself trigger Impl setVariable.
Batches 63-64 keep setVariable OPEN on zero printed setVariable hits. Batches 63, 64
and 65 (and, for consistency, 66-68) must use ONE rule:

| Option | 65/3, 65/16, 65/27, 65/38 (currently REACHABLE, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 63/10, 63/24, 64/4, 64/13, 64/22, 64/31 (currently OPEN, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 66/10, 67/8, 68/1 and other no-variable setVariable rows (currently OPEN) |
|--------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| D1 — batch-65 current rule (setViewModel implies setVariable dispatch) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | stay OPEN under (xv) |
| D2 — strict mechanical rule (only a printed setVariable call, or a printed generated body, triggers Impl setVariable) | revert to OPEN | stay OPEN | stay OPEN |
| D3 — generic-framework-callback rule (framework/generated dispatch always runs setVariable) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED — but this conflicts with (xv) and the merger must reconcile D3 with (xv) explicitly |

This pass applies none of D1/D2/D3 to change a label: the four batch-65 REACHABLE
setVariable rows are left exactly as written and flagged here, and the 63/64 rows are
not edited (outside this group). A printed generated Impl setVariable/setViewModel
body would settle the direction mechanically and supersede this table.

## Judging convention used in these files (recorded, NOT decided — merger must pick one rule
project-wide and apply it to every databinding batch)
Generated *Binding/*BindingImpl source and DataBinderMapperImpl are NOT in final_java_only
and were NOT printed, so NO row in these files is SOURCE: every REACHABLE row is INFERRED,
even where the app-side inflate/setViewModel call site itself is printed, because the step
from DataBindingUtil.inflate to the Impl is generated-mapper dispatch and the step from a
base-class call to the Impl override is generated virtual dispatch. This follows batch 67's
convention (and batches 62-64/68-70 on the same points) and is applied uniformly to 65-67:
- Impl <clinit> / both <init> overloads / base <init> / executeBindings / invalidateAll:
  REACHABLE INFERRED on a printed inflate of the exact layout (mapper instantiation and
  generated-constructor rebind are the standard generated pattern, body not printed).
- hasPendingBindings: OPEN everywhere — no printed app or framework caller in the printed
  corpus; claiming a framework call without a printed generated/framework body would be
  invention.
- onFieldChange / setVariable: REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where a variable is actually set in
  printed app code (a setViewModel call for a layout declaring that variable) and the layout
  binds observables of it; where no variable is set (layouts with NO <data> block), both stay
  OPEN. This is the batch 67 rule.
- onChangeX (nested binding): REACHABLE INFERRED ONLY where the parent layout includes child
  X AND the child layout binds observables that change in use; where the child layout has no
  <data>/observables, onChangeX stays OPEN (batch 66 row 7, appbar).
- Base getViewModel: OPEN everywhere in these files (see convention (v) below) — the
  executeBindings read path reads the mViewModel field, not the getter, as far as can be
  told without the generated body.
- setLifecycleOwner: OPEN everywhere — zero hits in all 26 freshly fetched files.
- Impl$1/$2 callback numbering (batch 66 rows 36-39): by generated-source order, NOT
  bytecode-confirmed.
- OPEN rows carry confirmed_by INFERRED in the table only as the ceiling of what can be said
  without generated source / a saved full-repo grep; it is NOT a label claim and NOT evidence
  of absence. No row is UNREACHABLE in these files, because no saved full-repo grep was run.

## Caveats the merger must resolve (these do not change the proposed labels above)
1. Strict-entry: routes are anchored at fragment onCreateView / onViewCreated (standard
   lifecycle callbacks) and, for the detail pages, at DetailPagerAdapter.createFragment.
   The launcher -> AddTorrentActivity / DetailTorrentActivity step was NOT printed in this
   pass, so under a strict printed-launcher-entry rule ALL REACHABLE rows in all three
   batches revert to OPEN.
2. confirmed_by per-row vs whole-route: these files use per-row INFERRED for generated
   dispatch (above). No SOURCE row exists to flip here, so a whole-route convention changes
   nothing in these files; labels are unaffected either way.
3. Binding-variant config gate (batch 65 rows 18-39, convention (iv)): the LargeLandNotouch
   and Sw600dpLand Impls are instantiated only under matching device configurations. Both
   variant layouts were freshly fetched and DO exist in the tree (see source list), and the
   same fragment inflates R.layout.fragment_add_torrent_info, so a route exists under a
   matching configuration. Under a single fixed portrait-phone standard config these 22 rows
   (18-39 minus the 4 OPEN hasPendingBindings/setLifecycleOwner rows among them: REACHABLE
   variant rows are 18-21, 23-25, 27-32, 34-36, 38-39) revert to OPEN (or
   closed_by_configuration) — merger must decide the standard-config rule. Batch 60's
   equivalent variant rows must be judged under the same rule.
4. Binding getViewModel convention (v): rows 65/6, 66/12, 66/24, 67/10, 67/22 are OPEN here.
   Prior batches 65-66 proposals marked them REACHABLE INFERRED via the executeBindings read
   path, while batches 58/63-64/67 left them OPEN. Merger must pick ONE rule for all binding
   getters (including batch 80 row 23 getDirName and batch 84 row 40 getBuildProgress, same
   family): adopting the executeBindings-read-path rule flips exactly these five rows in
   65-67 to REACHABLE INFERRED, nothing else.
5. hasPendingBindings / onFieldChange / setVariable for no-variable layouts: batch 66 rows
   5/7/8/10 (DetailTorrent Impl) are OPEN here under the batch-67 rule, whereas the prior
   batch-66 proposal marked them REACHABLE as generic framework callbacks. If the merger
   adopts the generic-framework-callback rule, rows 66/5, 66/8, 66/10 flip to REACHABLE
   INFERRED (row 66/7 onChangeAppbar stays OPEN on either rule unless the merger also
   accepts a no-observable child callback, since the appbar layout binds nothing).
6. Room finalize, TorrentDao_Impl $N mapping, and migration gates do NOT arise in batches
   65-67; no decision is needed for those here.
7. Batch 67 row 40 (TrackerList onFieldChange) and batch 68 row 1 (TrackerList Impl
   setVariable) are companion rows for the same no-variable binding and must be judged
   together at merge (both OPEN here / in batch 68).

### Table D — setVariable direction contradiction, batches 63 / 64 / 65 (NOT decided)

Contradiction, flagged never decided: batch 65 treats Impl setVariable as REACHABLE via
"generated dispatch point behind binding.setViewModel", which is mechanically backwards
— in generated code setVariable CALLS the typed setter (setViewModel), not vice versa,
so a printed binding.setViewModel() call does not by itself trigger Impl setVariable.
Batches 63-64 keep setVariable OPEN on zero printed setVariable hits. Batches 63, 64
and 65 (and, for consistency, 66-68) must use ONE rule:

| Option | 65/3, 65/16, 65/27, 65/38 (currently REACHABLE, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 63/10, 63/24, 64/4, 64/13, 64/22, 64/31 (currently OPEN, variable-set layouts, printed setViewModel only, zero printed setVariable hits) | 66/10, 67/8, 68/1 and other no-variable setVariable rows (currently OPEN) |
|--------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| D1 — batch-65 current rule (setViewModel implies setVariable dispatch) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | stay OPEN under (xv) |
| D2 — strict mechanical rule (only a printed setVariable call, or a printed generated body, triggers Impl setVariable) | revert to OPEN | stay OPEN | stay OPEN |
| D3 — generic-framework-callback rule (framework/generated dispatch always runs setVariable) | stay REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED | flip to REACHABLE INFERRED — but this conflicts with (xv) and the merger must reconcile D3 with (xv) explicitly |

This pass applies none of D1/D2/D3 to change a label: the four batch-65 REACHABLE
setVariable rows are left exactly as written and flagged here, and the 63/64 rows are
not edited (outside this group). A printed generated Impl setVariable/setViewModel
body would settle the direction mechanically and supersede this table.

## Why every row is OPEN (not UNREACHABLE proposed, not REACHABLE proposed)

1. **Generated source is absent from the upload.** Every row is a generated `*Binding` static (`bind`/`inflate`). The generated `*Binding`, `*BindingImpl`, and `databinding.DataBinderMapperImpl` sources are not in the tree, so no definition and no generated dispatch step can be printed from them in this pass.
2. **Direct-call absence does not close a generated row.** The freshly fetched saved grep proves **no direct app-code call** to any batch 5–7 `bind`/`inflate` static in the app Java tree, and its positive controls prove the grep was not empty (DataBindingUtil.inflate in 30 files; setContentView in the three activities above). That proves app code goes through `DataBindingUtil`. It does NOT prove generated code never calls these statics, and a grep over app source cannot rule out generated dispatch.
3. **The flip to UNREACHABLE rests on an unprinted premise.** It requires the premise that generated mapper code constructs binding objects directly (via constructors / `getDataBinder`) and never routes through the base `bind`/`inflate` statics. That premise is not printed in the grep file, in any audit, or in either layout fetched here. The batch-10 audit applied exactly this reasoning to identical-shape rows (`ItemTorrentDownloadableFileBinding` / `ItemTorrentListBinding` / `ItemTrackersListBinding` / `TagsListItemBinding`) and left them OPEN ("DataBindingUtil request exists; generated bind dispatch absent" → OPEN). Flipping batches 5–7 while batch 10 stays OPEN on the same ground would bake in the inconsistency the batches 1–12 verification flags for merge. The merger must reconcile that inconsistency project-wide, not in these batch files.
4. **Task rule.** Never flip OPEN to UNREACHABLE without a saved full-repo grep. No saved full-repo grep was run in this pass, and the saved grep above is an app-tree grep, not a full-repo grep for generated code (generated code is absent from the tree it searched). Therefore no row is proposed UNREACHABLE, even though the plan says UNREACHABLE and the direct-call grep points that way.
5. **No row is REACHABLE either.** No printed direct call names any row's static, and no printed generated dispatch names any row's static. A `DataBindingUtil` request for the row's layout proves the layout is bound; it does not prove the base `bind`/`inflate` static runs, because the request goes through the mapper/Impl path whose dispatch is unprinted.
6. **Binding-variant gap (per row, undecided).** Even where a DataBindingUtil request for a layout is printed, nothing printed selects WHICH of the 6 overloads (bind vs inflate; with vs without the `Object` DataBindingComponent argument; with vs without parent/attachToParent) the framework or mapper would invoke, if it invoked a base Binding static at all. The 6 overloads of one class therefore cannot be distinguished on this evidence, and no single overload can be promoted while its siblings stay OPEN on the same evidence.
7. **Child bindings do not change this.** Two batch-5 classes have no direct DataBindingUtil site at all: `AddTorrentInfoBinding` is a child binding of the requested `fragment_add_torrent_info` layout (fresh include, above), and `DetailTorrentAppbarBinding` is a child binding of the requested `fragment_detail_torrent` layout (fresh include, above). Their creation, if any, is by generated parent-Impl code, which is unprinted. They are OPEN on the same ground as the directly requested classes, not on a weaker ground — and the include does not make them REACHABLE, because the parent Impl dispatch is unprinted.

## confirmed_by convention in this file

- `INFERRED` for every OPEN row, following the batch-2 / batch-5 merge-ready convention: at best an inference, because the generated source is absent. It does NOT mean the row is close to closed; it means the ceiling of what can be said without generated source or a saved full-repo grep covering generated code.
- No row uses `DYN`, `FW-MEMORY`, or `UNVERIFIED` as `confirmed_by`. No row is `SOURCE`: no definition or call site for any row's method was freshly printed in this pass (the audits and grep are printed evidence about the absence of direct calls and the presence of layout requests, not call sites for the rows themselves).
- Undecided conventions flagged per row (never decided here): **generated-mapper dispatch** (does the mapper/Impl ever call a base `bind`/`inflate` static?); **binding-variant** (which overload, if any); **strict-entry** (see below); **binding getViewModel** (no getter row in these batches — not applicable); **finalize** (no finalize row — not applicable); **$N mapping** (no anonymous/inner-class row — not applicable); **migration gate** (no Room migration row — not applicable).

## Strict-entry

OPEN rows assert no route, so no entry step is claimed for any row. Strict-entry does not change an OPEN verdict. It matters only if a row is later flipped: any future REACHABLE flip for a row in these batches would additionally need its launcher → host-activity / dialog-show / fragment-attach step printed fresh (none of those steps was freshly printed in this pass, except the two layout includes above, which are not entry steps). Do not apply a strict-entry revert to OPEN rows — they are already OPEN.

## confirmed_by convention in this file

- SOURCE = every link in the cited chain is a printed app-code line, or a standard
  framework lifecycle/entry step whose app-side registration or call is printed.
  Direct internal calls from such an anchor stay SOURCE. This is the PER-ROW
  convention, not the whole-route convention used in some earlier batches;
  labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger
  adopts the whole-route convention. This convention is recorded, NOT decided here.
- INFERRED = the chain crosses an unprinted dispatch — WorkManager worker
  instantiation/doWork dispatch, AlarmManager/PendingIntent broadcast delivery,
  dynamic broadcast registration delivery, Rx/Handler dispatch, or the JVM
  class-initialiser rule — or the method itself is a synthetic lambda /
  `<clinit>` / framework-instantiated `<init>`. Downstream internal calls of an
  INFERRED-dispatched method stay INFERRED in this file (worker rule, following
  the batch 14 correction precedent). This convention is recorded, NOT decided here.
- No row is flipped to UNREACHABLE on absence: no saved full-repo grep was run.

## confirmed_by convention in this file

Same as batch 71 in this pass, recorded NOT decided:
- SOURCE = every link is a printed app-code line, or a standard framework
  lifecycle/entry step whose app-side registration or call is printed.
  A method directly called from a SOURCE anchor stays SOURCE. Per-row convention,
  not the whole-route convention; labels unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would
  change at merge under the whole-route convention.
- INFERRED = the chain crosses an unprinted dispatch — Rx subscription /
  method-reference dispatch, session-listener dispatch, Handler/Completable
  dispatch, or the JVM class-initialiser rule — or the method is a synthetic
  lambda, a `<clinit>`, or a framework-instantiated `<init>` / anonymous
  listener. Downstream internal calls of an INFERRED-dispatched method stay
  INFERRED (worker rule, following the batch 14 correction precedent).
- No row is flipped to UNREACHABLE on absence: no saved full-repo grep was run.

## confirmed_by convention in this file

SOURCE = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source
(including a printed listener body that calls it); INFERRED = the method is
reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch (DialogFragment
lifecycle, view-click dispatch, Rx/ViewModelProvider dispatch, JVM class
initialisation) where the registration or show site is printed. This is the
PER-ROW convention, not the whole-route convention used in some earlier batches;
labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger
adopts the whole-route convention. This convention is recorded, NOT decided here.

## Entry trunk (printed in the prior session)

- Launcher entry is printed: `AndroidManifest.xml:43` MainActivity, MAIN/LAUNCHER at `:53–55`. MainActivity-anchored rows therefore carry **no** strict-entry caveat.
- `MainActivity.onCreate:150` obtains `BaseAlertDialog.SharedViewModel` via `ViewModelProvider`; `MainActivity:764` calls `BaseAlertDialog.newInstance(...)` and `:772` shows it; `MainActivity:407` subscribes `dialogViewModel.observeEvents()`.
- `AddFeedActivity` is manifest-declared exported with SEND/VIEW intent filters (`AndroidManifest.xml:179–200`); its `onCreate:61/:63` calls `AddFeedDialog.newInstance(...)` and `:66` shows it (freshly printed in the batch 74 session). AddFeedDialog in turn shows both dialog groups below, so the ClipboardDialog and BaseAlertDialog rows are entry-closed through AddFeedActivity, not only through MainActivity.
- `Utils.showManageAllFilesWarningDialog` is called from `MainActivity:180` (freshly printed). See rows 39–40 for why that does **not** close the ManageAllFilesWarningDialog rows at API 27.

## Caveats for the merger

- **FileItem (rows 25–36) is the whole OPEN set apart from rows 39–40.** No
  file-list adapter or other caller file printed in the prior or this session
  names `FileItem` — CORRECTED 2026-11-01: fresh prints show BOTH
  TorrentContentFileItem (TorrentContentFileItem.java:32, extends FileItem) and
  DownloadableFileItem (DownloadableFileItem.java:29, extends FileItem) extend
  FileItem, with printed super calls (see table rows 25-27, 30, 33).
  The highest-value follow-up is a saved full-repo grep for `FileItem`
  (constructor, CREATOR, and `putParcelable`/Bundle use sites) plus a print of
  whichever file-list adapter, if any, uses it. Do not reuse the batch 82
  Parcelable convention to upgrade rows 27/35 without a printed parceling context.
- **ManageAllFilesWarningDialog (rows 39–40, and batch 74 row 1)** is a printed
  API-27 gate lead, not a verdict, for the reason in row 39. The merger must
  run the saved full-repo grep before labelling these rows either way.
- **Lambda numbering** (`$0–$3` in BaseAlertDialog, `$0–$1` in ClipboardDialog)
  is by source order and plan-descriptor match, **not bytecode-verified**.
  A swap within each group would not change any label.
- **Strict-entry:** no caveat for rows 1–24 and 37–38 — launcher entry
  (manifest MAIN/LAUNCHER) and the AddFeedActivity SEND/VIEW entry are both
  printed, and the dialog show sites are printed. Rows 25–36 and 39–40 are
  OPEN regardless of entry rule.
- **Not claimed:** no build, sampler, full-repo grep, or C1 check was run
  (no full local checkout; git clone of the private repo fails in this VM
  with no credential device — named missing check, not passed). No push was
  made. Nothing was edited in `graph_def.py`, `out/`, `PROGRESS_V2`,
  `AGENT_NOTES`, `tools/`, or another agent's files.

## Caveats for the merger

- **No OPEN rows remain.** Rows 1 and 9 were CLOSED 2026-11-01 (round 2) to UNREACHABLE closed_by_api_level on the saved full-tree grep over all 252 app Java files (`~/workspace/b727374-openclose2-src/full_grep_apigate_20261101.txt`): row 1 + batch 73 rows 39–40 — sole newInstance caller Utils:713, gated off at API 27; row 9 — sole launcher registration PermissionManager:61–67 and sole launch :100–104, both gated on SDK_INT >= R. Either row flips back to REACHABLE on a printed non-gated caller/registration. Row 23 was CLOSED in round 1 (see revision note). The round-1 partial-corpus grep (165-166 files) is superseded for these rows by the round-2 full-tree grep.
- **API-27 method-vs-branch distinction (rows 4, 12, 13, 17, 20):** a method whose body returns early or branches off at API 27 is still REACHABLE if its call site runs at API 27; only a method whose *call site itself* never runs at API 27 is a `closed_by_api_level` candidate (rows 1 and 9, still OPEN pending the grep). The merger should apply this distinction consistently to batch 45/115/118 rows.
- **Lambda numbering** (`PermissionManager $0/$1`) is by source order and plan-descriptor match, **not bytecode-verified**. A swap would not change any label, but `$0` vs `$1` matters for the row 9 gate argument, since only `$0`'s registration is gated — the descriptor match (`$0` takes `ActivityResult`, `$1` takes `Map`) is what pins `$0` to the gated StartActivityForResult lambda.
- **Strict-entry:** no caveat for rows 2–15 and 28–40 (manifest launcher, SEND, and SEND/VIEW entries are all freshly printed this pass). TorrentNotifier rows 16–27 (TorrentEngine/SchedulerReceiver-anchored) revert to OPEN under a strict launcher-entry rule, because the TorrentService → TorrentEngine step and the SchedulerReceiver alarm setup were not freshly printed in this pass (they rest on batch 71/72 prints, flagged as leads only).
- **FragmentCallback convention (viii):** row 32 is SOURCE on a printed dialog-side call through the interface cast, as in batches 82/86 (printed `finish()` callers) — not contract alone as in batch 108 row 30. The merger must pick one convention for all FragmentCallback rows.
- **Not claimed:** no build, sampler, full-repo grep, or C1 check was run. Build: **not run**. C1: **not run** (Mac after push, never claimed passed). Main-agent spot-check (1 in 5) and `py_compile` are outstanding. Pushed 2026-11-01 as 5e0983f (UNVERIFIED, before [L]) (pushed UNVERIFIED before [L] at user request 2026-11-01). Nothing was edited in `graph_def.py`, `out/`, `PROGRESS_V2`, `AGENT_NOTES`, `tools/`, or another helper's file. No other batch was touched.

## Lambda note (AddFeedDialog)

Descriptors distinguish most lambdas; individual $n-to-button mapping inside initAlertDialog is NOT verified from bytecode:

- $0 (Z)V: the only field-initialiser lambda, `AddFeedDialog` onFocusChanged `:112–113`, registered onCreateDialog `:207`, removed onDestroyView `:215`. By elimination this is $0.
- $1 (DialogInterface,int,KeyEvent)Z: onResume key listener `AddFeedDialog:146–157`, BACK + ACTION_DOWN → onBackPressed `:151`. Signature matches exactly.
- $2, $3 (View)V: initLayoutView click listeners, `AddFeedDialog:250` (end-icon → expandableLayout.toggle) and `:252` (clipboardButton → showClipboardDialog), in source order.
- $4–$7 (View)V: the four button click lambdas inside the onShow body, `AddFeedDialog:279` (edit → updateChannel), `:280` (delete → deleteFeedDialog), `:283` (add → addChannel), `:286–287` (cancel → finish CANCEL). Which $n is which button is NOT verified; the group route is the same for all four.
- $8 (DialogInterface)V: the setOnShowListener body itself, `AddFeedDialog:275–288`, registered `:275`; it installs the four button listeners above.
- $9 (BaseAlertDialog$Event)V: subscribeAlertDialog event lambda, `AddFeedDialog:303–326` (POSITIVE → deleteChannel `:313`; NEGATIVE → dismiss). Rx dispatch INFERRED.
- $10 (ClipboardDialog$Item)V: subscribeAlertDialog item lambda, `AddFeedDialog:325–332` (TAG_CLIPBOARD_DIALOG → handleUrlClipItem `:327`). Rx dispatch INFERRED.

## Audit note on the Rx-body setters (applied uniformly in the table)

Rows 6, 8, 9, 11 (setAutoDownload, setFilter, setName, setRegexFilter) are all INFERRED, not SOURCE, because their only printed app-code call sites are inside the Rx subscribe body `AddFeedViewModel:155–161` (row 24's lambda), and Rx dispatch is not printed. Each also has a two-way layout binding (`dialog_add_feed_channel.xml`: name `:37`, autoDownload `:80`, filter `:106`, regexFilter `:155`) — generated binding code is likewise unprinted, so the binding route does not change the confirmed_by either. Row 12 setUrl stays SOURCE: it also has non-Rx call sites (`AddFeedViewModel:84` and `AddFeedDialog:337`). Row 10 setNotDownloadImmediately is INFERRED on a different ground (generated two-way binding only, no app-code caller at all — see row 10). **Boundary flag, not decided:** if the merger counts a directly-printed call inside an Rx body as SOURCE (call site printed, dispatch only INFERRED for the lambda itself), rows 6, 8, 9, 11 and 16 flip to SOURCE and the counts become SOURCE 30 / INFERRED 10, with no label change.

- confirmed_by convention used here (per-row, as in the input proposal):
  SOURCE = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app
  source (including a printed listener body that calls it, and a lifecycle
  method whose show/registration step is printed); INFERRED = the method is
  reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch (DialogFragment
  lifecycle, view-click/key dispatch, Rx dispatch, JVM class initialisation)
  where the registration or show site is printed. Under the whole-route
  INFERRED convention used in some earlier batches (e.g. batch 14), all
  REACHABLE rows would flip to INFERRED; labels are unaffected. That
  convention choice is NOT decided here.

  SOURCE = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app
  source (including a printed listener body that calls it); INFERRED = the
  method is reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch

  unaffected. That convention choice is NOT decided here.

`new ViewModelProvider(this)` / `provider.get(AddTorrentViewModel.class)`; the two
fragments obtain the same ViewModel the same way (FilesFragment onViewCreated,
InfoFragment onViewCreated:101).

## confirmed_by convention in this file

- `SOURCE` = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source
  (including a printed construction site for `<init>` rows, and a printed listener
  body that calls the method).
- `INFERRED` = the method is reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch
  (activity/fragment lifecycle, menu/back dispatch, LiveData/Rx, TabLayoutMediator
  attach, View-click/adapter callbacks, data-binding Observable dispatch, JVM class
  initialisation) where the registration, show, or construction site is printed.
  This is the per-row convention, not the whole-route convention used in some earlier
  batches; labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the
  merger adopts the whole-route convention.
- No row uses `DYN`, `FW-MEMORY`, or `UNVERIFIED` as `confirmed_by`.

## Strict-entry caveat — CLOSED in this pass (with one nuance)

The prior proposal's strict-entry caveat (launcher/intent step into AddTorrentActivity
not printed) is closed by the fresh manifest print: AddTorrentActivity is exported
with SEND and VIEW intent filters (AndroidManifest.xml:62-72+). The activity →
pager → fragment chain is printed end-to-end above, so no row in this batch reverts
under an entry rule that accepts a manifest-registered external-intent entry.

this file does not decide it.

## confirmed_by convention in this file

- `SOURCE` = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source
  (including a printed construction site for `<init>` rows).
- `INFERRED` = the method is reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch
  (fragment lifecycle, ActivityResult dispatch, view-click/TextWatcher/listener
  dispatch, Rx dispatch, ViewModelProvider dispatch, generated data-binding
  `executeBindings` reads) where the registration, show, or construction site is printed.
  This is the per-row convention, not the whole-route convention used in some earlier
  batches; labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the
  merger adopts the whole-route convention.
- No row uses `DYN`, `FW-MEMORY`, or `UNVERIFIED` as `confirmed_by`.

## confirmed_by convention used here

- SOURCE: a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source, or a standard Android callback whose registration is printed (Activity/Fragment lifecycle, FragmentStateAdapter, View click listeners, anonymous-class instantiation inside a printed method).
- INFERRED: the route crosses a dispatch step NOT printed from its own source — RxJava subscribe/observe, data-binding Observable callbacks, Thread.start → lambda, AsyncTask.executeOnExecutor → doInBackground/onPostExecute, or JVM class initialisation (<clinit>).
- No row is flipped to UNREACHABLE on absence in the printed subset; rows with no printed caller stay OPEN pending a saved full-repo grep. No connector `search_code` 0-result was used as evidence of absence.

## confirmed_by convention used here

- SOURCE: a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source, or a standard Android callback whose registration is printed (Activity/Fragment lifecycle, RecyclerView.Adapter / ListAdapter + DiffUtil callbacks, View click listeners, anonymous-class instantiation inside a printed method).
- INFERRED: the route crosses a dispatch step NOT printed from its own source (RxJava, AsyncTask, data-binding Observable, JVM <clinit>, PermissionManager permission-result callbacks, framework Parcelable dispatch).
- No row is flipped to UNREACHABLE on absence in the printed subset; rows with no printed caller stay OPEN pending a saved full-repo grep. No connector `search_code` 0-result was used as evidence of absence.
